Also use when the user mentions 'plasma', 'Debye length', 'plasma frequency', 'MHD', 'magnetic confinement', 'tokamak', 'plasma instability', 'Alfven wave', 'Langmuir probe', 'ionosphere', or 'solar wind'. - **plasma-protein-risk-score**: Tier B lookup (human): what the paper reports, or simple arithmetic on values you bring; not the paper's model run on one person. Sums the published Cox, LASSO, and elastic-net protein betas (Nature Aging 2024 hip-fracture protein score) over per-standard-deviation protein values the user supplies, and computes heel-ultrasound bone density from BUA and SOS when both are given. The paper's protein values are log-transformed and normalised within the study cohort on a SomaScan assay, so one person's own values are not on that scale and the sum is not a validated personal risk. Use when the user… ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plasma-protein-risk-score) · ★1)
- **plasma-proteome-dementia-biomarkers**: Tier B lookup (human): what the paper reports, or simple arithmetic on values you bring; not the paper's model run on one person. Matches one person's protein symbols to the dementia plasma markers named with a direction in Bellomo et al., Nature Aging 2026 (doi:10.1038/s43587-026-01162-7). Use when the user mentions bPRIDE, GFAP, ITGAV, or differential dementia proteomics. Logistic coefficients stay in Supplementary Table 6, so they are not applied. Checkup labs do not add proteins. ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plasma-proteome-dementia-biomarkers) · ★1)
- **plasma-proteome-future-dementia**: Tier B lookup (human): what the paper reports, or simple arithmetic on values you bring; not the paper's model run on one person. Records plasma GFAP, NEFL, GDF15, and LTBP2 when the user supplies them, for the Guo et al. Nature Aging 2024 dementia profile. The protein risk score is a LightGBM model whose weights are not printed, so no risk score is calculated. Use when the user mentions this plasma proteomic dementia profile or those four proteins. Current medicines and checkup labs are context. The report does not say what to start or stop. ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plasma-proteome-future-dementia) · ★1)
- **plasma-proteomic-cellular-aging**: Tier B lookup (human): what the paper reports, or simple arithmetic on values you bring; not the paper's model run on one person. Applies the SomaScan cell-type clock coefficients that Ding and colleagues released with their Nature Medicine 2026 paper (cellage repository) to protein values the user supplies. The clocks expect protein levels z-scored against a whole cohort; neither the paper nor the repository gives reference means and standard deviations, so one person's own SomaScan file cannot be turned into valid inputs, and the paper prints no per-person example. Without cohort-standardiz… ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plasma-proteomic-cellular-aging) · ★1)
